电路分析基础 · 教材逐题Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis · Textbook exercise library

全书360道主习题清单，含范围外章节；现有65道完整题干与核验解析。计数按主习题，不把小问另算一题。360 numbered exercises across the full book, including chapters outside exam coverage. 65 currently have complete statements and checked solutions. Subparts are not counted as separate main exercises.

“清单已核对”只表示题号与来源页已收录，不表示题干已抄录或答案已完成。部分章的范围需要逐题确认。“Inventory checked” means that an ID and source page are indexed; it does not mean the statement or solution is complete. Coverage in partially taught chapters needs individual mapping.

1-1全书习题Book exercise已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第32页／PDF第41页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 32 / PDF p. 41. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

参考方向与实际方向：用正负号判断Reference and actual directions: interpret the sign

题目原文Transcribed question

已知在如题图1-1所示电路的电压、电流参考方向下，u=5 V，i=−2 A，请标出电压、电流的实际方向。With the voltage and current references in Fig. 1-1, u = 5 V and i = −2 A. Mark the actual voltage and current directions.

元件左端a、右端b；电流i参考箭头由a指向b；电压u左正右负。A two-terminal element has a at the left and b at the right. The reference current points from a to b, and voltage u is positive at a.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

参考方向是预先指定的坐标；正值表示与参考相同，负值表示相反。A reference is a chosen coordinate direction: a positive value agrees with it; a negative value reverses it.
这类题不需要另列电路方程。先把u解释为Vₐ−Vᵦ，再把i的负号解释为箭头反向。No additional circuit equations are needed. Interpret u as Vₐ−Vᵦ, then reverse the current arrow because i is negative.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

判断电压高低Determine the higher potential

u=Vₐ−Vᵦ=5 V>0，所以a端比b端高5 V；电压降的实际方向为a→b，即左高右低。u = Vₐ−Vᵦ = 5 V > 0, so a is 5 V above b. The actual voltage-drop direction is a→b.

u=Va−Vb=5V

反转负电流的箭头Reverse the negative current

i的参考方向为a→b，但i=−2 A，故实际电流为2 A，方向b→a。解答示意图给出了两个实际方向。The current reference is a→b, but i = −2 A. The actual current is therefore 2 A from b to a. The solution diagram shows both actual directions.

ia→b=−2A ⟺ ib→a=2A

实际方向示意：左端a电位较高、右端b较低，5 V电压降向右；实际2 A电流向左，由b流向a。Actual directions: a at left is higher than b at right. The 5 V voltage drop points right; actual 2 A current points left, from b to a.

电压：a高、b低，实际电压降a→b；电流：2 A，由b→a。Voltage: a is higher and b lower, with a→b voltage drop. Current: 2 A from b→a.

易错点Common errors

  • 负电流不是“没有电流”，而是与选定参考箭头相反。A negative current is not an absent current; it flows opposite the chosen reference arrow.
  • 电压的方向表示高低电位；不要把它误当作一定与电流同向。Voltage direction describes potential order and need not match the current direction.

核验依据Verification

独立参考反转核验：令Vₐ=5 V、Vᵦ=0，则Vₐ−Vᵦ=5 V；把电流坐标改为b→a，数值由−2变成+2 A。与书第332页/PDF341页1-1一致。Independent reference-reversal check: Vₐ = 5 V and Vᵦ = 0 reproduce u = 5 V. Reversing the current coordinate to b→a changes −2 A into +2 A. This matches answer 1-1 on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341.

1-2全书习题Book exercise已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第32页／PDF第41页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 32 / PDF p. 41. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

电荷求电流：正弦函数求导Current from charge: differentiate the sinusoid

题目原文Transcribed question

已知通过某二端元件的电荷q(t)=2sin(3t) C，t>0，求电流i(t)。The charge through a two-terminal element is q(t) = 2sin(3t) C for t > 0. Find i(t).

原书1-2完整题干，电荷为2sin(3t) C，时间范围t>0。The complete original 1-2 statement: q(t) = 2sin(3t) C for t > 0.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

电流定义i=dq/dt；复合函数求导；C/s=A。Current definition i = dq/dt, the chain rule and C/s = A.
给的是电荷而不是电流。求电流只需对时间求导，正弦求导后的系数还要乘以内部的3。The given quantity is charge. Differentiate it with respect to time, including the inner factor 3.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

写出定义Apply the definition

电流是沿同一参考方向的电荷变化率，单位为库仑每秒。Current is the rate of charge change in the same reference direction, measured in coulombs per second.

i(t)=dq(t)dt

应用链式法则Apply the chain rule

2sin(3t)求导后是2×3cos(3t)，其中3来自3t对t的导数。Differentiating 2sin(3t) gives 2×3cos(3t), where 3 is the derivative of the inner argument 3t.

i(t)=2·3cos(3t)=6cos(3t)A,t>0

i(t)=6cos(3t) A，t>0。i(t) = 6cos(3t) A, t > 0.

易错点Common errors

  • 不要漏掉链式法则中的3。Do not omit the chain-rule factor 3.
  • q是有符号的累计电荷，不要求在所有时刻为正。q is signed accumulated charge; it need not be positive at every instant.

核验依据Verification

独立积分核验：∫₀ᵗ6cos(3τ)dτ=2sin(3t)=q(t)−q(0)。另在多个时间点对原始q做中心差分，与6cos(3t)相符，误差小于10⁻⁸ A。与书第332页/PDF341页1-2一致。Independent integral check: ∫₀ᵗ6cos(3τ)dτ = 2sin(3t) = q(t)−q(0). Central differences of the original q at multiple times agree with 6cos(3t) to less than 10⁻⁸ A. This matches answer 1-2 on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341.

1-3全书习题Book exercise已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第32页／PDF第41页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 32 / PDF p. 41. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

指数电荷求导：常数消失，指数不变Exponential charge: constants vanish and the exponent remains

题目原文Transcribed question

已知通过某二端元件的电荷q(t)=(10+5e^(−3t)) C，t>0，求通过此元件的电流。The charge through a two-terminal element is q(t) = (10+5e^(−3t)) C for t > 0. Find the current through it.

原书1-3完整题干，指数清楚为−3t；q(t)=(10+5e^(−3t)) C。The complete original 1-3 statement, clearly showing exponent −3t in q(t) = (10+5e^(−3t)) C.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

电流i=dq/dt；指数函数链式法则；电荷守恒。i = dq/dt, the chain rule for exponentials and charge conservation.
10 C是常数项，其导数为0；e^(−3t)求导只增加系数−3，不会把原指数改成别的数。The constant 10 C differentiates to zero. Differentiating e^(−3t) multiplies it by −3 without changing its exponent.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

对原题逐项求导Differentiate the original statement term by term

严格采用题干指数−3t：常数10的导数为0，5e^(−3t)的导数为−15e^(−3t)。Use the original exponent −3t. The derivative of 10 is zero and that of 5e^(−3t) is −15e^(−3t).

i(t)=ddt(10+5e−3t)=−15e−3tA

用总电荷变化排除错误指数Reject a wrong exponent using total charge change

q(0⁺)=15 C，q(∞)=10 C，所以从0到∞的总电流积分必须为−5 C。正确式积分为−15/3=−5 C；附录−15e^(−5t)的积分仅−3 C，不能还原原题。q(0⁺) = 15 C and q(∞) = 10 C, so total integrated current must be −5 C. The correct expression integrates to −15/3 = −5 C; the appendix expression −15e^(−5t) integrates to only −3 C and fails to reconstruct the given charge.

∫0∞i(t)dt=−153=−5C=q(∞)−q(0+)

i(t)=−15e^(−3t) A，t>0。书后答案的指数−5t与原题不一致，应按题干−3t。i(t) = −15e^(−3t) A for t > 0. The appendix exponent −5t conflicts with the original statement; use −3t.

易错点Common errors

  • 求导会改变前面的系数，不会把e^(−3t)改为e^(−5t)。Differentiation changes the coefficient, not e^(−3t) into e^(−5t).
  • 不能仅因书后印有答案就覆盖原题和积分守恒。Do not override the original statement and charge balance solely because an appendix answer is printed.

核验依据Verification

独立双重核验：对原q做多点中心差分，误差小于10⁻⁸ A；正确电流从0到∞的积分为−5 C，与15 C降到10 C完全相符。高分辨率核对题干第32页/PDF41页为−3t，附录第332页/PDF341页印为−5t，确认为附录指数错误。Two independent checks: central differences of the original q agree to less than 10⁻⁸ A, and integrating current to infinity gives −5 C, exactly the fall from 15 C to 10 C. High-resolution inspection confirms −3t in the statement on printed p. 32/PDF p. 41 but −5t in the appendix on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341; the appendix exponent is wrong.

1-4全书习题Book exercise已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第32页／PDF第41页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 32 / PDF p. 41. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

电荷波形到电流与功率：斜率和能量符号From charge to current and power: slopes and energy signs

题目原文Transcribed question

设通过题图1-2(a)所示元件的电荷波形如题图1-2(b)所示。若单位正电荷由a端移至b端时获得的能量为2 J，求流过元件的电流i(t)及元件的功率p(t)，并画出i(t)与p(t)的波形。The charge waveform through the element in Fig. 1-2(a) is shown in Fig. 1-2(b). A unit positive charge gains 2 J when moving from terminal a to b. Find current i(t) and element power p(t), and draw both waveforms.

a在左、b在右，i参考方向a→b，u左正右负。q由0到1 s按5t² C增加到5 C；1到2 s保持5 C；2到3 s线性降至0。a is left, b right, reference i is a→b and u is positive at a. q rises as 5t² C from 0 to 1 s, stays at 5 C from 1 to 2 s, then falls linearly to 0 at 3 s.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

i=dq/dt；电压为单位正电荷的电位能差；关联方向下p吸收=ui；分段函数单侧极限。i = dq/dt, voltage as potential-energy difference per unit charge, absorbed power p = ui under passive references, and one-sided limits of piecewise functions.
先从“正电荷获得能量”判断b比a电位高，得到u=−2 V；再逐段读取q的斜率求i，最后按原图关联参考方向相乘得到吸收功率。First interpret the gained energy: b is above a in potential, so u = −2 V. Then differentiate each charge segment and multiply by u using the original passive references.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

把获得的能量换成图示电压Convert gained energy into the referenced voltage

单位正电荷从a到b获得2 J，故Vᵦ−Vₐ=2 V；图示u=Vₐ−Vᵦ，所以u=−2 V。A unit positive charge gains 2 J from a to b, so Vᵦ−Vₐ = 2 V. The drawn voltage is u = Vₐ−Vᵦ = −2 V.

u=Va−Vb=−2V

分段写q并求导Write and differentiate the charge pieces

0<t<1时q=5t²；1<t<2时q=5；2<t<3时q=15−5t。对应斜率分别为10t、0、−5，单位A。For 0<t<1, q = 5t²; for 1<t<2, q = 5; for 2<t<3, q = 15−5t. Their slopes are 10t, 0 and −5 A.

i(t)={10tA,0<t<10,1<t<2−5A,2<t<3

求吸收功率并画波形Compute absorbed power and draw the waveforms

i从u正端流入，因此p=ui=−2i。第一段负功率表示元件提供能量；第三段正功率表示吸收能量。解答图中的虚线只提示跳变，不代表额外冲激。Current enters u’s positive terminal, so p = ui = −2i. Negative first-segment power means energy delivery; positive third-segment power means absorption. Dashed jump guides in the solution plot do not represent impulses.

pabs(t)={−20tW,0<t<10,1<t<210W,2<t<3

说明分段交界和图外延拓State the corner and extension conventions

在t=1、2、3处，q的斜率发生跳变，通常不为导数单独指定一个值，应按左右极限画图。q本身连续，因此电流没有冲激。若按图中3 s后的零值水平线延拓，则t>3时i=p=0。At t = 1, 2 and 3 the charge slope jumps, so use one-sided limits rather than assigning a unique derivative value. Charge is continuous, hence no current impulse. If the zero-charge horizontal continuation after 3 s is used, i = p = 0 for t > 3.

两幅精确分段波形：i从0线性升到10 A的左极限，在1 s降到0，2 s降到−5 A，3 s回0；p从0线性降到−20 W的左极限，在1 s回0，2 s升到10 W，3 s回0。虚线为跳变提示，开圆为单侧极限。Exact piecewise plots: i rises linearly from 0 to the 10 A left limit, drops to 0 at 1 s, then to −5 A at 2 s and returns to 0 at 3 s. p falls linearly to the −20 W left limit, returns to 0 at 1 s, rises to 10 W at 2 s and returns to 0 at 3 s. Dashed lines guide jumps; open circles show one-sided limits.

u=−2 V。i：0<t<1为10t A，1<t<2为0，2<t<3为−5 A；p吸收依次为−20t W、0、10 W。波形见解答图；交界点用单侧极限。u = −2 V. i is 10t A for 0<t<1, 0 for 1<t<2 and −5 A for 2<t<3. Absorbed power is respectively −20t W, 0 and 10 W. See the solution plots; use one-sided limits at corners.

易错点Common errors

  • “电荷获得能量”给出Vᵦ−Vₐ>0，图示u却是Vₐ−Vᵦ。The gained energy implies Vᵦ−Vₐ > 0, whereas the drawn u is Vₐ−Vᵦ.
  • q的恒定平台对应零电流，不是5 A。A constant charge plateau means zero current, not 5 A.
  • q连续但斜率跳变，只产生电流跳变，不产生电流冲激。Continuous q with a slope jump creates a current jump, not an impulse.

核验依据Verification

独立电荷/能量核验：三段电流积分为+5、0、−5 C，总计0，重建q由0回到0；吸收能量为−10、0、+10 J，总计0，等于uΔq。各非交界时间点对原q作差分也相符。附录第332页/PDF341页列出的各段电流、功率与此一致。Independent charge/energy checks: integrated segment currents are +5, 0 and −5 C, totaling zero and reconstructing the return of q to zero. Absorbed energies are −10, 0 and +10 J, totaling uΔq = 0. Differentiation checks away from corners also agree. The appendix segment currents and powers on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341 match.

1-5指定作业Assigned已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第32页／PDF第41页。范围状态：指定作业（指定小问优先）。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 32 / PDF p. 41. Coverage: assigned exercise; stated subparts take priority.

指定要求：1-5Assigned requirement: 1-5

参考方向与吸收功率Reference directions and absorbed power

题目原文Transcribed question

已知在题图1-3所示电压、电流参考方向下，元件A的电压、电流分别为u=-3 V、i=5 A，元件B的电压、电流分别为u=5 V、i=-2 mA，试求两元件吸收的功率。Under the voltage and current reference directions shown in Fig. 1-3, element A has u = -3 V and i = 5 A, while element B has u = 5 V and i = -2 mA. Find the power absorbed by each element.

A：电流参考方向由左向右，电压左正右负；B：电流参考方向由右向左，电压右正左负。两者电流箭头都流入电压正参考端。A: current points left to right, with voltage positive on the left. B: current points right to left, with voltage positive on the right. Both current references enter the positive voltage-reference terminal.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

电压和电流参考方向；关联参考方向；功率符号与单位换算。Voltage and current reference directions; passive sign convention; power signs and unit conversion.
先看箭头是否流入标“+”的端子，再把带符号的数值代入公式。First check whether the current arrow enters the terminal marked +, then substitute the signed values.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

确认参考方向Check the reference directions

A和B的电流参考方向都流入电压正参考端，因此两者吸收功率公式均为p=ui。For both A and B, the current reference enters the positive voltage-reference terminal, so absorbed power is p = ui.

pabs=ui

计算ACalculate A

A的电压为负，按给定参考方向直接代入。负吸收功率表示该元件实际提供功率。Substitute the signed voltage for A. A negative absorbed power means that the element delivers power.

pA=(−3)(5)=−15W

计算B并统一单位Calculate B and convert units

把-2 mA换成-0.002 A。B的吸收功率为-0.010 W，即-10 mW。Convert -2 mA to -0.002 A. B absorbs -0.010 W, or -10 mW.

pB=5(−2×10−3)=−0.010W=−10mW

A吸收-15 W（提供15 W）；B吸收-10 mW（提供10 mW）。A absorbs -15 W (delivers 15 W); B absorbs -10 mW (delivers 10 mW).

易错点Common errors

  • 不要因i为负而翻转箭头后仍代入原负值，避免重复改符号。Do not reverse the arrow for a negative current and then keep the original negative value; that would change the sign twice.
  • mA与A相差1000倍。mA and A differ by a factor of 1000.

核验依据Verification

按关联参考方向独立计算，并与书第332页（PDF341页）1-5参考答案逐项一致。Independently calculated using the passive sign convention and matched item by item to answer 1-5 on printed p. 332 (PDF p. 341).

1-6全书习题Book exercise已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第32页／PDF第41页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 32 / PDF p. 41. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

局部电路KCL：允许未知电流取负Local KCL: allow a negative unknown current

题目原文Transcribed question

求题图1-4所示部分电路中的电流i₁和i₂。Find currents i₁ and i₂ in the partial circuit of Fig. 1-4.

左节点2 A流入、5 A流出；4 A源从左到右；下方电阻i₁参考向右。右节点8 A流出、6 A流入；i₂参考从右下方流入。At the left node 2 A enters and 5 A leaves. A 4 A source points left to right, as does bottom reference i₁. At the right node 8 A leaves and 6 A enters; i₂ enters from the lower right.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

基尔霍夫电流定律；按参考箭头代数求和；整体边界电流守恒。Kirchhoff’s current law, algebraic currents in their reference directions and boundary-current conservation.
每个节点写一条“流入=流出”。未知量先按题目箭头保留，求出负值后再解释实际方向。Write entering = leaving at each node. Keep each unknown in the given reference, then interpret its actual direction after solving.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

左节点求i₁Use the left node for i₁

2 A流入；5 A、4 A和参考i₁流出，所以2=5+4+i₁。2 A enters; 5 A, 4 A and reference i₁ leave, giving 2 = 5+4+i₁.

2=5+4+i1⇒i1=−7A

右节点求i₂Use the right node for i₂

4 A、参考i₁、6 A及i₂流入，8 A流出。代入i₁=−7 A，得到i₂=5 A。4 A, reference i₁, 6 A and i₂ enter; 8 A leaves. Substituting i₁ = −7 A gives i₂ = 5 A.

4+i1+6+i2=8⇒i2=5A

i₁=−7 A（实际由右向左7 A）；i₂=5 A（按原图从右下方流入右节点）。i₁ = −7 A, actually 7 A right to left. i₂ = 5 A, entering the right node from the lower right as drawn.

易错点Common errors

  • i₁在右节点参考流入，代入时必须保留−7 A，不能改成+7而不改箭头。At the right node i₁ is referenced inward; retain −7 A unless you also reverse its arrow.
  • 4 A是连接两个节点的内部电流，整体边界KCL中会相消。The 4 A source is internal to the two-node region and cancels in the boundary KCL.

核验依据Verification

独立整体边界核验：外部总流入2+6+5=13 A，总流出5+8=13 A；内部4 A和i₁均相消。另独立联立两节点方程得到同样的−7、5 A。与附录第332页/PDF341页1-6一致。Independent boundary check: external inflow 2+6+5 = 13 A equals outflow 5+8 = 13 A; internal 4 A and i₁ cancel. An independent simultaneous solve of both node equations gives −7 and 5 A. This matches appendix 1-6 on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341.

1-7指定作业Assigned已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第32页／PDF第41页。范围状态：指定作业（指定小问优先）。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 32 / PDF p. 41. Coverage: assigned exercise; stated subparts take priority.

指定要求：1-7Assigned requirement: 1-7

已知支路电流，结合KCL与KVL求未知电流Use KCL and KVL with known branch currents

题目原文Transcribed question

题图1-5所示电路为某电路的一部分，已知i₁=6 A，i₂=-2 A，求图中电流i。The circuit in Fig. 1-5 is part of a larger circuit. Given i₁ = 6 A and i₂ = -2 A, find the current i shown in the figure.

三个左端口分别经3 Ω电阻接入上、中、下节点，i₁、i₂、i₃参考方向均向右。上、中节点间6 Ω，中、下节点间18 Ω，上、下节点间右支路12 Ω；待求i沿12 Ω向下。Three left ports connect through 3 Ω resistors to the upper, middle, and lower nodes; i₁, i₂, and i₃ all point right. There are 6 Ω between upper and middle nodes, 18 Ω between middle and lower nodes, and 12 Ω in the right branch from upper to lower, with unknown i pointing downward.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

KCL、KVL、电阻关联参考方向。KCL, KVL, and the resistor passive sign convention.
已有两个注入电流，先用KCL把内部支路电流都写成i，再对两条上到下的路径列一次KVL；不用求三个左端口的电压。With two injection currents already known, use KCL to express the internal branch currents in terms of i, then equate the voltage drops along the two upper-to-lower paths. The left-port voltages are unnecessary.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

上节点列KCLApply KCL at the upper node

设6 Ω中的电流向下为i₆。流入的6 A分成i和i₆，所以i₆=6-i。Let i₆ point downward through 6 Ω. The 6 A entering the upper node splits into i and i₆.

i6=6−i

中节点列KCLApply KCL at the middle node

设18 Ω中的电流向下为i₁₈。i₂=-2 A已经包含参考方向的信息，因此i₁₈=i₆+i₂=4-i。Let i₁₈ point downward through 18 Ω. The value i₂ = -2 A already includes its reference-direction sign, so i₁₈ = i₆ + i₂ = 4 - i.

i18=i6+i2=4−i

同起点、同终点的电压相等Equate voltage drops between the same nodes

上到下经12 Ω的电压降为12i；经6 Ω和18 Ω的总电压降为6(6-i)+18(4-i)。The voltage drop from upper to lower is 12i through 12 Ω and 6(6-i) + 18(4-i) through the other path.

12i=6(6−i)+18(4−i)

解方程并解释方向Solve and interpret the direction

整理得36i=108，故i=3 A。正号表示实际方向与图中向下的箭头一致。Rearranging gives 36i = 108, so i = 3 A. The positive result agrees with the downward arrow.

i=3A

i=3 A，沿12 Ω电阻向下。i = 3 A, downward through the 12 Ω resistor.

易错点Common errors

  • i₂=-2 A表示中间端口实际流出2 A，KCL中保留负号。i₂ = -2 A means 2 A actually leaves the middle port; retain the negative sign in KCL.
  • 三个3 Ω电阻的电流已给定或可由整体KCL确定，求i不需要先算它们的压降。The port currents are given or follow from overall KCL; their 3 Ω voltage drops are unnecessary for finding i.

核验依据Verification

第二种方法：以下节点为0 V独立列节点方程，得上节点36 V、中节点18 V，故i=36/12=3 A。内部电流i₆=3 A、i₁₈=1 A满足全部KCL；书第332页（PDF341页）答案亦为3 A。Independent nodal check with the lower node at 0 V gives upper 36 V and middle 18 V, hence i = 36/12 = 3 A. Internal currents i₆ = 3 A and i₁₈ = 1 A satisfy every KCL equation. The answer on printed p. 332 (PDF p. 341) is also 3 A.

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第33页／PDF第42页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 33 / PDF p. 42. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

复杂极性图：先赋节点电位A polarity-heavy figure: assign node potentials first

题目原文Transcribed question

求题图1-6所示电路中的电压u₁、u₂和u₃。Find voltages u₁, u₂ and u₃ in Fig. 1-6.

菱形节点T顶、B底、L左、R右。外10 V源T正B负；左下10 V为L正B负；右下6 V为R正B负。u₁为L正R负，u₂为R正T负，u₃为T正L负。Diamond nodes are T top, B bottom, L left and R right. Outer 10 V is positive at T relative to B; lower-left 10 V at L relative to B; lower-right 6 V at R relative to B. u₁ = V_L−V_R, u₂ = V_R−V_T and u₃ = V_T−V_L.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

节点电位；电压等于正端电位减负端电位；KVL。Node potentials, voltage as positive-terminal potential minus negative-terminal potential, and KVL.
将底节点设为0 V，可直接读出T、L、R三个电位。随后所有未知电压只需按图示极性相减，避免在斜边上反复判断符号。Set the bottom node to zero and directly read T, L and R. Obtain each unknown by subtracting the negative-terminal potential from the positive-terminal potential.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

选择参考节点Choose a reference node

令V_B=0。三个已知电压分别给出V_T=10 V、V_L=10 V、V_R=6 V。Choose V_B = 0. The given voltages yield V_T = 10 V, V_L = 10 V and V_R = 6 V.

VB=0,VT=10,VL=10,VR=6(V)

按三个未知量的正负端相减Subtract using each unknown polarity

u₁左正右负；u₂右节点为正、顶节点为负；u₃顶节点为正、左节点为负。u₁ is positive at L and negative at R. u₂ is positive at R and negative at T. u₃ is positive at T and negative at L.

u1=VL−VR=4V,u2=VR−VT=−4V,u3=VT−VL=0

u₁=4 V，u₂=−4 V，u₃=0 V。u₁ = 4 V, u₂ = −4 V and u₃ = 0 V.

易错点Common errors

  • 原图斜向正号旋转后外形近似×，应结合两端极性看清位置。The rotated plus signs resemble ×; identify which end of each diagonal branch is positive.
  • 两端都有10 V对地电位时，端间电压是0，不是20 V。Two terminals each at 10 V relative to ground have zero voltage between them, not 20 V.

核验依据Verification

独立KVL核验：中央三边环路u₁+u₂+u₃=4−4+0=0。节点差值同时重现外10 V、左下10 V及右下6 V三个已知量。与附录第332页/PDF341页1-8一致。Independent KVL check: u₁+u₂+u₃ = 4−4+0 = 0 around the central triangle. The potentials also reproduce all three given 10 V, 10 V and 6 V differences. This matches appendix 1-8 on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341.

1-9全书习题Book exercise已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第33页／PDF第42页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 33 / PDF p. 42. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

各元件功率：先统一节点，再判断关联方向Element power: solve nodes, then use passive signs

题目原文Transcribed question

电路如题图1-7所示，已知i₁=2 A，i₃=−3 A，u₁=10 V，u₄=−5 V，试求各元件吸收的功率。In Fig. 1-7, i₁ = 2 A, i₃ = −3 A, u₁ = 10 V and u₄ = −5 V. Find the power absorbed by each element.

四元件：1在上方，u₁左正右负而i₁向左；2在左竖支路，u₂上正下负；3在中竖支路，u₃上负下正而i₃向下；4在右竖支路，u₄上正下负。底部为共节点。Element 1 is on top, with u₁ positive left and i₁ leftward. Element 2 is on the left, u₂ positive at top. Element 3 is central, u₃ positive at bottom and i₃ downward. Element 4 is on the right, u₄ positive at top. The bottom is common.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

KCL；节点电位；关联方向p吸收=ui，非关联方向p吸收=−ui；总功率守恒。KCL, node potentials, absorbed p = ui for passive references or −ui for opposite references, and total power balance.
不能对所有元件机械使用ui。先由u₄和u₁求两个上节点电位，再用KCL补齐支路电流，逐个按“流入高参考端”的规则求吸收功率。Do not blindly use ui for every element. Determine the two upper potentials from u₄ and u₁, recover currents by KCL, then evaluate each absorbed power with its reference polarity.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

设底部为0 V求节点电位Set the bottom to zero and recover potentials

令右上节点B、左上节点A。u₄=V_B=−5 V，u₁=V_A−V_B=10 V，故V_A=5 V；于是u₂=5 V、u₃=0−V_B=5 V。Let B be upper right and A upper left. u₄ = V_B = −5 V and u₁ = V_A−V_B = 10 V, so V_A = 5 V. Therefore u₂ = 5 V and u₃ = 0−V_B = 5 V.

VB=−5,VA=5,u2=u3=5(V)

用KCL补齐元件2和4电流Use KCL for elements 2 and 4

元件2从A向下电流为i₂=i₁=2 A。定义元件4向下为i₄，B点流出代数和i₁+i₃+i₄=0，所以i₄=1 A。Element 2 carries i₂ = i₁ = 2 A downward from A. Define i₄ downward; the outgoing sum at B is i₁+i₃+i₄ = 0, giving i₄ = 1 A.

i2=2A,i4=−(2−3)=1A

逐个计算吸收功率Compute each absorbed power

1和3的图示电流均从电压负参考端流入，故用−ui；2和4用ui。元件3应为−5×(−3)=15 W。For 1 and 3 the drawn current enters the negative voltage-reference terminal, so use −ui. For 2 and 4 use ui. Element 3 absorbs −5×(−3) = 15 W.

p1=−u1i1=−20W,p2=u2i2=10W,p3=−u3i3=15W,p4=u4i4=−5W

用功率守恒审查答案Audit the answer with power balance

四元件吸收功率之和−20+10+15−5=0。1和4总共提供25 W，2和3总共吸收25 W。附录把元件3印成5 W会使总和变成−10 W，不满足守恒。The sum −20+10+15−5 = 0. Elements 1 and 4 deliver 25 W, while 2 and 3 absorb 25 W. The appendix value 5 W for element 3 instead gives −10 W total, violating conservation.

∑k=14pk=0

按元件1、2、3、4顺序：−20 W、10 W、15 W、−5 W。负值表示提供功率。附录第三个值5 W有误。For elements 1, 2, 3 and 4: −20 W, 10 W, 15 W and −5 W. Negative values mean delivered power. The appendix’s third value 5 W is wrong.

易错点Common errors

  • 元件3的u₃上负下正、i₃向下，属于非关联参考方向。For element 3 u₃ is positive at the bottom while i₃ points down, so the references are nonpassive.
  • 有符号的i₃=−3 A要直接代入，不能丢掉负号。Substitute the signed i₃ = −3 A without discarding its negative sign.

核验依据Verification

独立按物理支路首末节点计算p=(V起−V终)i，四项仍为−20、10、15、−5 W；全部节点KCL和总功率残差均为0。题干和完整极性原图第33页/PDF42页清楚；附录第332页/PDF341页印第三项5 W，与节点电位和功率守恒同时冲突。Independent physical branch calculation p = (V_start−V_end)i gives −20, 10, 15 and −5 W, with zero KCL residual at every node and zero power residual. The original statement and full polarity diagram on printed p. 33/PDF p. 42 are clear. The appendix’s third value 5 W on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341 conflicts with both node potentials and power conservation.

1-10全书习题Book exercise已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第33页／PDF第42页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 33 / PDF p. 42. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

对地电压求支路电流：分清4 Ω与4 SFrom node voltage to current: distinguish 4 Ω from 4 S

题目原文Transcribed question

电阻元件两端的对地电压如题图1-8所示，求通过各电阻元件的电流i。The terminal voltages relative to ground are shown in Fig. 1-8. Find current i through each resistive element.

(a)左端3 V、右端1 V，电阻4 Ω，i参考向左。(b)左端u₁、右端u₂，标注电导4 S，i参考向左。(a) Left terminal 3 V, right 1 V, resistance 4 Ω, with i leftward. (b) Left terminal u₁, right u₂, conductance 4 S, again with i leftward.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

欧姆定律i=ΔV/R=GΔV；S为西门子，1 S=1 Ω⁻¹；按电流箭头选电压差。Ohm’s law i = ΔV/R = GΔV, Siemens 1 S = 1 Ω⁻¹, and the voltage drop along the current reference.
两图电流都由右向左，因此电压差都取“右端减左端”。(a)给电阻，除以4；(b)给电导，乘以4。Both currents are referenced right to left, so use right-terminal minus left-terminal voltage. Divide by 4 for the resistance in (a), but multiply by 4 for the conductance in (b).
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

(a)电阻形式(a): resistance form

沿i向左的电压降为1−3=−2 V，除以4 Ω得到−0.5 A，实际电流向右。The voltage drop along leftward i is 1−3 = −2 V. Dividing by 4 Ω gives −0.5 A, actually rightward.

i=1−34=−0.5A

(b)电导形式(b): conductance form

图中4 S是电导，不是4 Ω。等效电阻为1/4 Ω；因此i=G(u₂−u₁)=4(u₂−u₁)。The original 4 S is conductance, not 4 Ω. Its equivalent resistance is 1/4 Ω, so i = G(u₂−u₁) = 4(u₂−u₁).

G=4S,R=14Ω,i=4(u2−u1)Awhen voltages are in V

(a) i=−0.5 A；(b) i=4(u₂−u₁) A（u₁、u₂以V计，原图给的是4 S电导）。(a) i = −0.5 A. (b) i = 4(u₂−u₁) A for voltages in volts; the original figure gives 4 S conductance.

易错点Common errors

  • S和Ω表示互为倒数的物理量，不能将(b)当成4 Ω。S and Ω describe reciprocal quantities; do not read (b) as 4 Ω.
  • 对地电位不能直接当电阻端电压，先沿参考箭头做两端差。A single ground-referenced potential is not the resistor voltage; take the terminal difference along the reference.

核验依据Verification

独立量纲与功率核验：(a)吸收功率(−2 V)(−0.5 A)=1 W；(b)S×V=A，且p=4(u₂−u₁)²≥0；以等效R=0.25 Ω计算得到相同电流。高分辨率原图确认4 S，附录第332页/PDF341页两问均正确。Independent dimensional and power checks: (a) absorbs (−2 V)(−0.5 A) = 1 W. (b) has S×V = A and p = 4(u₂−u₁)² ≥ 0; using equivalent R = 0.25 Ω gives the same current. High-resolution inspection confirms 4 S, and both appendix answers on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341 are correct.

1-11全书习题Book exercise已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第33页／PDF第42页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 33 / PDF p. 42. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

有分流的电阻链：从末端逐级累加电流A tapped resistor chain: accumulate current from the bottom

题目原文Transcribed question

电阻电路中各点对地电压及各支路电流如题图1-9所示，求各电阻的阻值。Ground-referenced node voltages and branch currents are shown in Fig. 1-9. Find all resistor values.

30 V经R₁至24 V节点，该处向右流出10 mA；R₂下接12 V节点，向右流出15 mA；R₃下接6 V节点，向右流出20 mA；R₄下接0 V，向下电流5 mA。30 V connects through R₁ to 24 V, which has 10 mA outgoing. R₂ leads to 12 V with 15 mA outgoing; R₃ to 6 V with 20 mA outgoing; R₄ to 0 V carries 5 mA downward.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

KCL累加分流；电阻端电压为相邻节点差；mA换算。KCL with outgoing taps, adjacent-node voltage differences and mA conversion.
这不是四只电阻同流的简单串联链。每层有分流，应从已知最底5 mA向上逐层加上流出的电流，再用各自压降除以各自电流。The four resistors do not share one current because taps draw current. Start from the given 5 mA at the bottom, add each outgoing tap upward, then divide each voltage drop by its own current.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

从R₄向上做KCLApply KCL upward from R₄

R₄电流为5 mA；R₃须另供20 mA，故25 mA；R₂须另供15 mA，故40 mA；R₁须另供10 mA，故50 mA。R₄ carries 5 mA. R₃ must also supply 20 mA, so it carries 25 mA; R₂ adds 15 mA to reach 40 mA; R₁ adds 10 mA to reach 50 mA.

I4=5,I3=25,I2=40,I1=50(mA)

用相邻节点压降求RUse adjacent-node drops for R

各段压降依次为6、12、6、6 V，除以各段电流50、40、25、5 mA。The respective voltage drops are 6, 12, 6 and 6 V. Divide by the corresponding currents 50, 40, 25 and 5 mA.

R1=60.05=120Ω,R2=120.04=300Ω,R3=60.025=240Ω,R4=60.005=1200Ω

R₁=120 Ω，R₂=300 Ω，R₃=240 Ω，R₄=1200 Ω。R₁ = 120 Ω, R₂ = 300 Ω, R₃ = 240 Ω and R₄ = 1200 Ω.

易错点Common errors

  • 每个节点有支路分流，不能令四只电阻电流相同。The node taps draw current, so the four resistor currents are unequal.
  • V/mA直接给出kΩ；若答案写Ω，先把mA化为A。V/mA gives kΩ; convert mA to A when reporting Ω.

核验依据Verification

独立逐节点回代KCL全部为0；源输入30×0.05=1.5 W，电阻共吸收0.96 W，三个外接端口带走24×0.01+12×0.015+6×0.02=0.54 W，和为1.5 W。高分辨率附录第332页/PDF341页R₄实际印为1200 Ω，与结果一致。Independent node-by-node substitution gives zero KCL residual. Input power is 30×0.05 = 1.5 W; resistors absorb 0.96 W and the three external ports take 24×0.01+12×0.015+6×0.02 = 0.54 W, totaling 1.5 W. High-resolution appendix inspection confirms R₄ = 1200 Ω on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341, matching the result.

1-12全书习题Book exercise已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第33页／PDF第42页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 33 / PDF p. 42. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

理想电压源并联电阻：电压固定，源电流调整A resistor across an ideal voltage source: voltage fixed, source current adjusts

题目原文Transcribed question

电路如题图1-10所示，其中Uₛ为理想电压源，试判断当外电路不变，仅电阻R变化时，将会引起电路中哪条支路电流的变化。In Fig. 1-10, Uₛ is an ideal voltage source. With the external circuit unchanged, determine which branch current changes when only R changes.

理想电压源Uₛ上正下负，与R并联，共同接到右侧外电路端口；端口u上正下负，外电流i由上端向右流出。An ideal top-positive voltage source Uₛ is parallel with R and the right external port. Port u is positive at the top and external current i leaves the upper terminal to the right.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

理想电压源固定端电压；并联电压相同；KCL；固定外电路的工作点。Fixed ideal-source voltage, common parallel voltage, KCL and the operating point of an unchanged external circuit.
Uₛ直接钳定外端口电压u=Uₛ。R变化会改变自身电流，但外电路输入电压未变，因此其原工作电流i不变；理想源会补足R电流变化。Uₛ directly fixes port voltage u = Uₛ. Changing R changes its own current. The external circuit retains the same voltage and its operating current i; the ideal source supplies the extra resistor current.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

先固定端口电压Fix the port voltage first

理想电压源与外端口并联，故u始终等于Uₛ。对工作点确定的固定外电路，i由这个固定端电压决定，因此不随R变化。The ideal voltage source is across the external port, so u remains Uₛ. For an unchanged external circuit with a determined operating point, i is fixed by this voltage and does not change with R.

u=Us,iexternal unchanged

写出R支路及电源支路电流Write resistor and source currents

定义R中向下电流i_R，电源从下向上供出电流i_s。则i_R=Uₛ/R，节点KCL为i_s=i+i_R。一般R变化时，i_R和i_s都相应变化；端口i不变。Define i_R downward through R and i_s upward supplied by the source. Then i_R = Uₛ/R and KCL gives i_s = i+i_R. In general both i_R and i_s change as R changes, while external i remains fixed.

iR=UsR,is=i+UsR

补充附录简答省略的源电流Complete the appendix’s abbreviated statement

附录只写“电阻R支路的电流变化”，说明了R支路的变化，但若把电源支路也计入题目，电源电流必须同步调整，不能宣称其恒定。若Uₛ=0等退化情况，具体变化由上述公式判断。The appendix states only that the resistor-branch current changes. This identifies R’s response, but if the source branch is included, its current must adjust as well. The formulas also cover degenerate cases such as Uₛ = 0.

一般R变化会使R支路电流i_R=Uₛ/R变化，理想电压源供出电流i_s=i+i_R也随之调整；外电路电流i不变。In general changing R changes i_R = Uₛ/R and also the ideal source’s supplied current i_s = i+i_R. External current i is unchanged.

易错点Common errors

  • 理想电压源固定的是电压，不是自身电流。An ideal voltage source fixes its voltage, not its current.
  • “外电路电流不变”不能推广成“源支路电流也不变”。Unchanged external current does not imply unchanged source current.

核验依据Verification

独立数值反例与功率核验：Uₛ=10 V，固定外负载10 Ω。R从10 Ω变到5 Ω时，外电流保持1 A；R电流1→2 A，源电流2→3 A。源功率20→30 W，恰等于外负载10 W加R功率10→20 W。附录第332页/PDF341页定性简答省略了这项源电流调整，记录中已补全。Independent numerical and power check: with Uₛ = 10 V and fixed external 10 Ω load, changing R from 10 to 5 Ω leaves external current at 1 A, increases resistor current from 1 to 2 A and source current from 2 to 3 A. Source power rises from 20 to 30 W, balancing the fixed 10 W external load plus 10 to 20 W in R. The qualitative appendix answer on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341 omits the source-current adjustment, which is included here.

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第33页／PDF第42页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 33 / PDF p. 42. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

含源支路特性：下降斜率的绝对值就是RA source-containing branch: resistance is the negative slope

题目原文Transcribed question

一段含源支路及其u-i特性如题图1-11所示，图中3条直线对应于电阻R的3个不同数值R₁、R₂、R₃，试根据该图判断各电阻的大小关系。A source-containing branch and its u-i characteristics are shown in Fig. 1-11. The three lines correspond to R₁, R₂ and R₃. Determine their ordering.

串联电压源Uₛ上正下负、上方电阻R，电流i向上并从正端口流出；端电压u上正下负。u-i图纵轴u、横轴i，R₁线水平，R₂缓降，R₃陡降，共同纵截距Uₛ。A top-positive source Uₛ is series with R above it. Current i flows upward and leaves the positive port; u is top-positive. The plot has u vertical and i horizontal: R₁ horizontal, R₂ gently descending, R₃ steeply descending, all intercepting Uₛ.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

KVL；含源支路端口特性；u-i图斜率du/di=−R。KVL, source-containing port characteristics and slope du/di = −R.
先按端口电流“流出正端”的参考方向写u=Uₛ−Ri。电阻越大，曲线随电流下降越快；不要套用无源电阻的正斜率。Because current leaves the positive port, the branch law is u = Uₛ−Ri. Greater resistance gives a steeper downward line; do not use the positive slope of a passive resistor reference.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

从原图写端口关系Write the port law from the figure

电流从源正端向上流过R，电阻压降为Ri，端口剩余电压为Uₛ−Ri。Current rises from the source’s positive terminal through R, dropping Ri. The remaining port voltage is Uₛ−Ri.

u=Us−Ri,dudi=−R

用斜率绝对值比较RCompare negative-slope magnitudes

R₁曲线水平，所以R₁=0；R₃比R₂下降更陡，因此R₃>R₂>0。R₁’s line is horizontal, giving R₁ = 0. R₃ descends more steeply than R₂, so R₃ > R₂ > 0.

R1=0<R2<R3

R₁=0，且R₁<R₂<R₃。R₁ = 0 and R₁ < R₂ < R₃.

易错点Common errors

  • 图的横轴为i、纵轴为u，斜率单位才是Ω；轴反过来则读的是电导。With i horizontal and u vertical, the slope has units Ω; reversed axes would describe conductance.
  • 此题电流流出正端口，特性斜率为−R，不能直接用+R。Here current leaves the positive port, so the slope is −R rather than +R.

核验依据Verification

独立固定正电流比较：三条线同截距Uₛ，若i>0，则u₁>u₂>u₃；由R=(Uₛ−u)/i即得R₁<R₂<R₃。再以有限差分检查Δu/Δi=−R。与附录第332页/PDF341页1-13一致。Independent fixed-current comparison: all lines share intercept Uₛ; for i > 0, u₁ > u₂ > u₃. Then R = (Uₛ−u)/i gives R₁ < R₂ < R₃. A finite-difference check also gives Δu/Δi = −R. This matches appendix 1-13 on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341.

1-14全书习题Book exercise已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第33页／PDF第42页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 33 / PDF p. 42. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

电流源网络：外节点KCL直接确定电阻压降Current-source network: outer-node KCL fixes resistor drops

题目原文Transcribed question

求题图1-12所示电路中的u_ae、u_be、u_cf、u_df、u_ef。Find u_ae, u_be, u_cf, u_df and u_ef in Fig. 1-12.

外矩形节点a左下、b左上、c右上、d右下，内部e左、f右。电流源a→b为1 A，b→c为2 A，a→d为3 A，d→c为4 A。电阻b-e为5 Ω、a-e为4 Ω、c-f为10 Ω、d-f为8 Ω、e-f为20 Ω。Outer nodes are a lower left, b upper left, c upper right, d lower right; inner e left and f right. Sources: a→b 1 A, b→c 2 A, a→d 3 A, d→c 4 A. Resistors: b-e 5 Ω, a-e 4 Ω, c-f 10 Ω, d-f 8 Ω, e-f 20 Ω.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

KCL；电阻电压u_xy=V_x−V_y；电流源方向；节点法与全电路功率守恒。KCL, u_xy = V_x−V_y, source directions, nodal analysis and full-network power conservation.
四个外节点各只连一只电阻，另两支路是已知电流源，因此每只外围电阻的电流可以直接由KCL确定。再到e点求20 Ω电流；无需先解大量未知电压。Each outer node has one resistor and two known current sources, so KCL directly determines each outer resistor current. Then use node e for the 20 Ω current, avoiding an unnecessary large voltage calculation.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

左侧a、b节点Use left nodes a and b

a向外流出1+3=4 A，故e→a为4 A，u_ae=−4×4=−16 V。b流入1 A、流出2 A，故e→b为1 A，u_be=−1×5=−5 V。At a, sources take 1+3 = 4 A outward, so e→a carries 4 A and u_ae = −4×4 = −16 V. At b, 1 A enters and 2 A leaves, so e→b is 1 A and u_be = −1×5 = −5 V.

uae=−16V,ube=−5V

右侧c、d节点Use right nodes c and d

c收到2+4=6 A，故c→f为6 A，u_cf=6×10=60 V。d收到3 A、流出4 A，故f→d为1 A，u_df=−1×8=−8 V。At c, sources deliver 2+4 = 6 A, so c→f carries 6 A and u_cf = 6×10 = 60 V. At d, 3 A enters and 4 A leaves, so f→d is 1 A and u_df = −1×8 = −8 V.

ucf=60V,udf=−8V

e节点求中央20 Ω压降Use node e for the central 20 Ω drop

e向a、b分别流出4 A、1 A，总共5 A，所以中央支路必须由f向e流入5 A。u_ef=−5×20=−100 V。e sends 4 A to a and 1 A to b, so 5 A must enter from f. Therefore u_ef = −5×20 = −100 V.

ie→f=−5A,uef=−100V

建立一组节点电位用于回代Construct node potentials for substitution

取V_e=0，可得V_a=−16、V_b=−5、V_f=100、V_c=160、V_d=92 V。它们重现五个所求电压以及每个电流源节点的KCL。With V_e = 0, obtain V_a = −16, V_b = −5, V_f = 100, V_c = 160 and V_d = 92 V. These reproduce all five requested differences and source-node KCL.

(Va,Vb,Vc,Vd,Ve,Vf)=(−16,−5,160,92,0,100)V

u_ae=−16 V；u_be=−5 V；u_cf=60 V；u_df=−8 V；u_ef=−100 V。u_ae = −16 V; u_be = −5 V; u_cf = 60 V; u_df = −8 V; u_ef = −100 V.

易错点Common errors

  • u_ae表示V_a−V_e；不能只把电阻压降大小当作答案。u_ae means V_a−V_e; the unsigned drop magnitude is insufficient.
  • d点电流源3 A流入、4 A流出，因此电阻实际电流必须由f流向d。At d the sources provide 3 A inward but 4 A outward, so the resistor current must run from f to d.

核验依据Verification

独立按原图搭建五节点电导矩阵，以有理数高斯消元求得V_a=−16、V_b=−5、V_c=160、V_d=92、V_f=100 V（V_e=0），全部节点残差精确为0。五电阻功率为5、64、360、8、500 W，总937 W；四源吸收功率−11、−330、−324、−272 W，总−937 W，全电路功率和0。与附录第332页/PDF341页1-14五项全部一致。An independent five-node conductance matrix stamped from the original topology and solved by exact rational Gaussian elimination gives V_a = −16, V_b = −5, V_c = 160, V_d = 92 and V_f = 100 V with V_e = 0; every nodal residual is exactly zero. Resistor powers 5, 64, 360, 8 and 500 W total 937 W. Source absorbed powers −11, −330, −324 and −272 W total −937 W, giving zero overall power. All five values match appendix 1-14 on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341.

1-15全书习题Book exercise已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第33页／PDF第42页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 33 / PDF p. 42. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

指定端电压反求电源：由固定电压逐层做KCLRecover the source from a target terminal voltage

题目原文Transcribed question

电路如题图1-13所示，欲使u_AB=5 V，问电压源电压uₛ应是多少？For Fig. 1-13, determine source voltage uₛ so that u_AB = 5 V.

B为底部公共节点、A为上节点。左源uₛ上正下负，其上端经1 Ω接A。A与中节点M之间5 V源上正下负；M经1 Ω接B。右支路A经1 Ω接N、N经1 Ω接B。10 A源由M向右流入N。B is the bottom common node and A the top node. The top-positive left source uₛ connects through 1 Ω to A. A top-positive 5 V source joins A to middle M; M connects through 1 Ω to B. On the right A connects through 1 Ω to N and N through 1 Ω to B. A 10 A source runs from M to N.

本题图在书第34页／PDF第43页。The source figure is on printed p. 34 / PDF p. 43.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

节点电位；理想电压源约束；KCL；按实际压降求源电压。Node potentials, ideal-source constraints, KCL and voltage drops.
目标u_AB先给出A电位，5 V源随即给出中节点电位。先对右中节点列KCL，再对A、M组成的超节点列KCL，可以直接反求左源。The target fixes A, and the 5 V source then fixes M. Apply KCL first at the right-middle node, then to the A–M supernode to recover the left source.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

固定A、M电位Fix A and M potentials

取V_B=0，目标u_AB=5 V给出V_A=5 V。5 V源要求V_A−V_M=5 V，因此V_M=0，中央下方1 Ω无电流。Choose V_B = 0. The target gives V_A = 5 V. The 5 V source imposes V_A−V_M = 5 V, so V_M = 0 and the center-bottom 1 Ω carries no current.

VA=5,VM=VA−5=0(V)

右中节点求V_NUse the right-middle node for V_N

10 A向N流入；从N流向A和B的电阻电流之和为10 A。The 10 A source enters N; the resistor currents from N to A and B total 10 A.

VN−51+VN−01=10⇒VN=7.5V

超节点求左源电压Use the supernode for the left source

A、M超节点向外流出：(5−uₛ)/1、(5−7.5)/1、0/1和10 A。令其代数和为0，解得uₛ=12.5 V。Outgoing currents of the A–M supernode are (5−uₛ)/1, (5−7.5)/1, 0/1 and 10 A. Their algebraic sum is zero, giving uₛ = 12.5 V.

(5−us)+(5−7.5)+0+10=0⇒us=12.5V

uₛ=12.5 V（原图上正下负）。uₛ = 12.5 V, positive at the top as drawn.

易错点Common errors

  • 10 A源由中央流向右侧，不能在N点按流出10 A列式。The 10 A source enters N from the center; do not count it as 10 A leaving N.
  • u_AB是A相对B的电压，参考节点可直接选B。u_AB is A relative to B, so B is a convenient reference.

核验依据Verification

独立原拓扑MNA回代uₛ=12.5 V，得到A=5、M=0、N=7.5 V，重现目标。四只电阻总耗散118.75 W；5 V源吸收50 W，左源提供93.75 W，10 A源提供75 W，吸收功率总和0。与第332页/PDF341页1-15的12.5 V一致。Independent MNA of the original topology with uₛ = 12.5 V gives A = 5, M = 0 and N = 7.5 V, reproducing the target. The four resistors dissipate 118.75 W; the 5 V source absorbs 50 W, the left source supplies 93.75 W and the 10 A source supplies 75 W, yielding zero total absorbed power. This matches 12.5 V in appendix 1-15 on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341.

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第33页／PDF第42页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 33 / PDF p. 42. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

串联电流源定电流：逐个判断吸收和提供Series current source: classify every element by passive power

题目原文Transcribed question

电路如题图1-14所示，若uₛ>0，iₛ>0，R>0，请指出电路中各元件是吸收功率还是提供功率。In Fig. 1-14, uₛ > 0, iₛ > 0 and R > 0. State whether each element absorbs or supplies power.

单回路：左电压源uₛ上正下负，顶端电阻R，右电流源iₛ向上。电流由右上向左穿过R，再从电压源正端向下流过电压源。One loop contains a top-positive left voltage source uₛ, upper resistor R and right current source iₛ pointing up. Current runs right to left through R, then downward into the voltage source’s positive terminal.

本题图在书第34页／PDF第43页。The source figure is on printed p. 34 / PDF p. 43.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

串联支路同流；电阻功率Ri²；电流流入正端为吸收；KVL。Equal series current, resistor power Ri², passive sign convention and KVL.
电流源把全回路电流固定为iₛ>0。顺着这个实际电流画方向，可以直接看出电压源的电流进入正端；电流源承担供给两者吸收功率。The current source fixes the loop current at positive iₛ. Following that actual direction shows current entering the voltage source’s positive terminal. The current source supplies the power absorbed by the other two elements.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

电阻一定吸收The resistor absorbs

R>0且iₛ>0，所以电阻耗散R iₛ²>0。R > 0 and iₛ > 0 give positive resistor dissipation R iₛ².

pR=Ris2>0

电压源也吸收The voltage source also absorbs

电流沿左支路向下，从uₛ正端流入，故p_Us=uₛiₛ>0。电压源不一定供电，本题明确在吸收功率。Current goes downward in the left branch and enters the positive uₛ terminal, giving p_Us = uₛiₛ > 0. This voltage source absorbs power.

pUs=usis>0

电流源提供两者所需功率The current source supplies both loads

右源上端电位为uₛ+Riₛ，电流却由下向上，因此其吸收功率为−(uₛ+Riₛ)iₛ<0，即提供相同大小的功率。The right source’s top potential is uₛ+Riₛ, but current rises from bottom to top. Its absorbed power is −(uₛ+Riₛ)iₛ < 0, so it supplies that magnitude.

pIs=−(us+Ris)is<0,pR+pUs+pIs=0

电阻R和电压源uₛ吸收功率；电流源iₛ提供功率。R and voltage source uₛ absorb power; current source iₛ supplies power.

易错点Common errors

  • 电源元件也可以吸收功率，不能只根据名称判断。A source element can absorb power; its name alone does not determine the power sign.
  • 先跟随iₛ的向上箭头，顶端电阻电流是向左，不是向右。Follow the upward iₛ arrow: current through the upper resistor is leftward.

核验依据Verification

独立符号功率平衡Riₛ²+uₛiₛ−(uₛ+Riₛ)iₛ恒等于0。另以uₛ=3 V、iₛ=2 A、R=4 Ω搭建原拓扑MNA，电阻/电压源/电流源吸收功率分别16、6、−22 W，节点方程及功率残差精确为0。与附录第332页/PDF341页1-16一致。The symbolic power sum Riₛ²+uₛiₛ−(uₛ+Riₛ)iₛ is identically zero. Independent original-topology MNA with uₛ = 3 V, iₛ = 2 A and R = 4 Ω gives absorbed powers 16, 6 and −22 W for resistor, voltage source and current source, with exact zero residuals. This matches appendix 1-16 on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341.

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第33页／PDF第42页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 33 / PDF p. 42. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

并联理想源的功率：电压源也会吸收Parallel ideal sources: a voltage source can absorb power

题目原文Transcribed question

电路如题图1-15所示，试验证该电路功率守恒。Verify power conservation for the circuit in Fig. 1-15.

三支路并联：左1 V电压源上正下负，中1 Ω电阻，右2 A电流源向上。Three parallel branches: a left top-positive 1 V source, central 1 Ω resistor and right 2 A source pointing up.

本题图在书第34页／PDF第43页。The source figure is on printed p. 34 / PDF p. 43.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

并联共电压；欧姆定律；KCL确定电压源电流；有符号功率守恒。Common parallel voltage, Ohm’s law, KCL for voltage-source current and signed power balance.
先用1 V源确定所有支路电压，再由电阻电流和2 A注入电流求左源电流。只有把电压源的吸收功率也计入才能验证守恒。Use the 1 V source to fix every branch voltage, then obtain the voltage-source current from resistor current and 2 A injection. Include the voltage source’s absorbed power in the balance.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

求电阻与电压源电流Find resistor and voltage-source currents

电阻上下电压1 V，向下电流1/1=1 A。右源向上注入2 A，因此剩余1 A从左电压源向下流回。The resistor has 1 V top to bottom and carries 1 A downward. The right source injects 2 A upward, so the remaining 1 A flows downward through the voltage source.

iR=1A,iV=2−1=1Adownward

按吸收功率统一记号Use absorbed power consistently

电阻吸收1 W；电压源电流进入正端，吸收1 W；电流源由低电位端向高电位端送2 A，吸收功率−2 W。The resistor absorbs 1 W. Current enters the voltage source’s positive terminal, so it also absorbs 1 W. The current source sends 2 A from lower to higher potential, giving −2 W absorbed.

pR=1,pV=1,pI=−2(W)

相加验证守恒Sum powers to verify conservation

全部吸收功率之和1+1−2=0；等价于电流源提供2 W，电阻与电压源各吸收1 W。Total absorbed power is 1+1−2 = 0. Equivalently, the current source supplies 2 W and the resistor and voltage source each absorb 1 W.

∑pabs=1+1−2=0

电阻吸收1 W，1 V电压源吸收1 W，2 A电流源提供2 W；总吸收功率为0，功率守恒。The resistor absorbs 1 W, the 1 V source absorbs 1 W and the 2 A source supplies 2 W. Total absorbed power is zero.

易错点Common errors

  • 不能遗漏1 V源的电流；理想电压源的电流由KCL决定。Do not omit the 1 V source current; KCL determines it.
  • 统一用吸收功率求和，提供功率应记负号。When summing absorbed power, record supplied power with a negative sign.

核验依据Verification

独立MNA解原三并联支路得到上节点1 V、电压源向下1 A；三元件功率1、1、−2 W，KCL/KVL及功率残差精确为0。附录第332页/PDF341页从1-16跳到1-18，未列1-17答案，本题据原图独立完成。Independent MNA of the original three parallel branches gives top potential 1 V and 1 A downward through the voltage source. Powers 1, 1 and −2 W give exact zero KCL/KVL and power residuals. The appendix on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341 skips from 1-16 to 1-18, so 1-17 has no listed answer and is independently solved from the original figure.

1-18全书习题Book exercise已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第34页／PDF第43页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 34 / PDF p. 43. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

理想源并联电阻网络：电压源功率取决于负载Ideal sources in parallel: voltage-source power depends on load

题目原文Transcribed question

题图1-16所示电路中，若电压源uₛ=10 V，电流源iₛ=1 A，试说明电压源和电流源是否一定都在提供功率。In Fig. 1-16, uₛ = 10 V and iₛ = 1 A. Explain whether both sources necessarily supply power.

左1 A电流源向上，中10 V电压源上正下负，右侧为电阻网络；三者跨接同一上下节点。An upward 1 A source, a top-positive 10 V source and a resistor network are all parallel across common upper and lower nodes.

本题图在书第34页／PDF第43页。The source figure is on printed p. 34 / PDF p. 43.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

理想电压源钳定电压；电阻网络等效电阻；KCL；源的功率正负。Ideal-source voltage clamping, equivalent network resistance, KCL and source power signs.
电压恒为10 V，因此向上的1 A源必定把电流送向高电位端，提供10 W。电压源补充还是吸收剩余电流，取决于电阻网络需求是否超过1 A。Voltage is fixed at 10 V, so the upward 1 A source always drives toward the higher potential and supplies 10 W. The voltage source supplies a deficit or absorbs a surplus depending on whether the resistor network requires more or less than 1 A.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

电流源一定提供10 WThe current source always supplies 10 W

电流源从下端向上端流1 A，下到上电位升高10 V，所以吸收功率为−10 W，即提供10 W。The current source carries 1 A from bottom to top across a 10 V rise, so it absorbs −10 W and supplies 10 W.

PIs,supplied=10×1=10W

用等效电阻表示负载需求Express the load demand by equivalent resistance

设正常工作的正电阻网络等效为R_eq>0，其向下电流I_R=10/R_eq。定义电压源向上供出电流I_V，则KCL给出I_V=10/R_eq−1。For an admissible positive equivalent resistance R_eq, the network draws downward I_R = 10/R_eq. Define voltage-source supplied current I_V upward; KCL gives I_V = 10/R_eq−1.

IR=10Req,IV=10Req−1,PV,supplied=10(10Req−1)

分三种负载判断电压源Classify the voltage source in three cases

R_eq<10 Ω时负载需超过1 A，电压源提供功率；R_eq=10 Ω时电压源电流为0；R_eq>10 Ω时1 A源供给过剩，电压源吸收功率。因此两源不一定都提供。If R_eq < 10 Ω, the load requires more than 1 A and the voltage source supplies power. At 10 Ω its current is zero. Above 10 Ω the current source supplies a surplus and the voltage source absorbs it. Thus both sources need not supply power.

电流源一定提供10 W；电压源不一定，随负载可提供、零功率或吸收功率，分界为R_eq=10 Ω。The current source always supplies 10 W. The voltage source may supply, exchange zero power or absorb power, with boundary R_eq = 10 Ω.

易错点Common errors

  • 电压源保持10 V，不意味着其电流或功率符号固定。Fixing 10 V does not fix the voltage-source current or its power sign.
  • 并联理想电压源和电流源时，剩余电流由电压源承担。With parallel ideal voltage and current sources, the voltage source carries the remaining current.

核验依据Verification

独立原拓扑MNA分别取R_eq=5、10、20 Ω，电压源提供功率分别10、0、−5 W；电流源始终提供10 W，电阻耗散20、10、5 W，三种情况功率总和均精确为0。与附录第332页/PDF341页“电流源提供功率，电压源不一定”一致。Independent original-topology MNA for R_eq = 5, 10 and 20 Ω gives voltage-source supplied powers 10, 0 and −5 W. The current source always supplies 10 W; load dissipation is 20, 10 and 5 W, with exact zero power residual in all cases. This matches the appendix statement on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341 that the current source supplies power while the voltage source need not.

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第34页／PDF第43页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 34 / PDF p. 43. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

电压源钳位与平衡桥：先求总负载电流Voltage clamp and balanced bridge: recover total load current

题目原文Transcribed question

求题图1-17所示电路中电流源和电压源提供的功率。Find the powers supplied by the current source and voltage source in Fig. 1-17.

左10 A源向上，经顶端1 Ω接20 V上正下负源的上节点。该20 V节点另接5 Ω对地；右桥为10 Ω/20 Ω左臂、20 Ω/40 Ω右臂，两中点用30 Ω相连。An upward 10 A source feeds a top 1 Ω into the positive node of a top-positive 20 V source. That node also connects to ground through 5 Ω and a bridge: 10 Ω over 20 Ω on the left, 20 Ω over 40 Ω on the right, joined at their midpoints by 30 Ω.

本题图在书第34页／PDF第43页。The source figure is on printed p. 34 / PDF p. 43.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

理想电压源固定负载电压；平衡桥；KCL；提供功率等于负吸收功率。Fixed load voltage, bridge balance, KCL and supplied power as negative absorbed power.
20 V源直接固定右侧负载电压。电流源的10 A全部先流过1 Ω，因而源上端比20 V节点再高10 V；右侧桥臂比例相同，30 Ω不取电流。The 20 V source fixes the right load voltage. All 10 A passes through 1 Ω first, lifting the current-source top by 10 V. The bridge ratios match, so the 30 Ω branch carries zero current.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

求电流源电压与提供功率Find current-source voltage and supplied power

1 Ω电流10 A，从左向右压降10 V；源上端电位为20+10=30 V。电流从下向上，因此提供30×10=300 W。The 1 Ω resistor carries 10 A left to right and drops 10 V. The current-source top is 20+10 = 30 V, so its upward current supplies 30×10 = 300 W.

Vcurrent source=20+10(1)=30V,PI,supplied=300W

平衡桥求负载总电流Find total load current using bridge balance

10/20=20/40，故桥中点等电位，30 Ω无电流。负载电流为20/5+20/30+20/60=4+2/3+1/3=5 A。Since 10/20 = 20/40, the midpoints are equipotential and the 30 Ω bridge has no current. Total load current is 20/5+20/30+20/60 = 4+2/3+1/3 = 5 A.

Iload=205+2010+20+2020+40=5A

KCL确定20 V源功率Use KCL for the 20 V source power

流入顶节点10 A，负载取5 A，剩余5 A向下流入20 V源正端。因此电压源吸收100 W，所问“提供功率”为−100 W。10 A enters the top node and the load takes 5 A. The remaining 5 A enters the 20 V source’s positive terminal downward. It absorbs 100 W, so its supplied power is −100 W.

IV=10−5=5A downward,PV,supplied=−20(5)=−100W

电流源提供300 W；电压源提供−100 W，即吸收100 W。The current source supplies 300 W; the voltage source supplies −100 W, meaning it absorbs 100 W.

易错点Common errors

  • 提供功率可以为负，负值应解释成吸收，不应擅自改成+100 W。Supplied power can be negative; interpret it as absorption rather than changing it to +100 W.
  • 30 Ω无电流来自桥臂比例相等，需要先检查平衡条件。The 30 Ω carries no current because the bridge ratios match; check that condition first.

核验依据Verification

独立保留30 Ω桥支路的完整MNA得到桥两中点均40/3 V、源顶节点30 V、电压源向下5 A。1 Ω耗散100 W，右负载共100 W，电压源吸收100 W，恰等于电流源提供300 W；全电路功率和0。与附录第332页/PDF341页1-19的300 W、−100 W一致。Independent full MNA retaining the 30 Ω bridge gives both midpoints 40/3 V, current-source top 30 V and 5 A down through the voltage source. The 1 Ω resistor dissipates 100 W, the right load 100 W and voltage source absorbs 100 W, balancing the 300 W supplied by the current source. This matches 300 W and −100 W in appendix 1-19 on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341.

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第34页／PDF第43页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 34 / PDF p. 43. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

短接电流源串联电阻：右侧仍注入同样电流Bypass a resistor in series with a current source

题目原文Transcribed question

电路如题图1-18所示，试判断当开关S由打开到闭合时，电路中的电压u和电流i是否发生变化。In Fig. 1-18, determine whether voltage u and current i change when switch S closes.

左1 A源向上，经左2 Ω到顶节点T；S并联在左2 Ω两端。中央T经2 Ω到C、再经4 Ω到底；右T经5 Ω到D、再经4 Ω到底。C、D间1 V源左正右负。u为中央上2 Ω上正下负；i为右下4 Ω向下。An upward left 1 A source connects through left 2 Ω to top T, with S bypassing that resistor. T connects through center 2 Ω to C, then 4 Ω to bottom; through right 5 Ω to D, then 4 Ω to bottom. A 1 V source is positive at C relative to D. u is across upper center 2 Ω, positive at T; i is downward through lower-right 4 Ω.

本题图在书第34页／PDF第43页。The source figure is on printed p. 34 / PDF p. 43.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

理想电流源固定注入电流；电流源串联电阻；超节点KCL；开关短接。Fixed current injection, a resistor in series with an ideal current source, supernode KCL and a shorting switch.
S只短接左源串联的2 Ω。无论开关状态，理想源向右侧网络注入的净电流仍为1 A；右侧元件和1 V约束均未改变，因此其唯一稳态解中的u、i不变。S bypasses only the resistor in series with the left source. The ideal source still injects 1 A into the right network in either state. With the right elements and 1 V constraint unchanged, u and i retain the same unique steady-state values.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

识别固定的端口注入Identify unchanged port injection

开路时1 A流经左2 Ω后到T；闭合时1 A改走开关到T，左2 Ω被短路。右侧收到的总电流始终1 A。With S open, 1 A passes through left 2 Ω to T. With S closed, it flows through the switch and the resistor is bypassed. The right network always receives 1 A.

用右侧超节点求C、DUse the right-side supernode for C and D

取底部0 V。C、D超节点向底部流出的电流之和等于注入1 A，因此(V_C+V_D)/4=1；1 V源给V_C−V_D=1。解得V_C=2.5、V_D=1.5 V。Take the bottom as zero. The C–D supernode sends the injected 1 A to ground, so (V_C+V_D)/4 = 1, while the source gives V_C−V_D = 1. Thus V_C = 2.5 V and V_D = 1.5 V.

VC+VD=4,VC−VD=1⇒VC=52, VD=32

计算共同的u、i作为核验Compute the shared u and i as a check

T点KCL为(V_T−2.5)/2+(V_T−1.5)/5=1，得V_T=51/14 V。故u=V_T−V_C=8/7 V，i=V_D/4=3/8 A，两种状态相同。KCL at T gives (V_T−2.5)/2+(V_T−1.5)/5 = 1, hence V_T = 51/14 V. Therefore u = V_T−V_C = 8/7 V and i = V_D/4 = 3/8 A in either switch state.

u=87V,i=38Afor both states

u和i均不变；两种状态都为u=8/7 V≈1.143 V，i=3/8 A=0.375 A。Neither u nor i changes. Both states give u = 8/7 V ≈ 1.143 V and i = 3/8 A = 0.375 A.

易错点Common errors

  • 闭合S短接的是左侧2 Ω，不是中央测u的2 Ω。S bypasses the left 2 Ω, not the central 2 Ω across which u is measured.
  • 右侧u、i不变，不代表电流源端电压和左电阻功率不变。Unchanged right-side u and i do not imply unchanged current-source voltage or left-resistor power.

核验依据Verification

分别以开路原节点及闭合后合并节点建立独立MNA，两次都得到T=51/14、C=5/2、D=3/2 V及u=8/7 V、i=3/8 A，全部KCL/KVL精确满足。左源端电压开路79/14 V、闭合51/14 V，相差2 V；左2 Ω耗散由2 W降为0，完整功率平衡两态均为0。与附录第332页/PDF341页1-20“不变”一致。Separate independent MNA models for the open topology and merged closed-switch node both give T = 51/14, C = 5/2 and D = 3/2 V, with u = 8/7 V and i = 3/8 A, exactly satisfying KCL/KVL. Current-source voltage changes from 79/14 to 51/14 V, and the bypassed resistor dissipation from 2 W to zero; full power balance holds in both states. This matches “unchanged” in appendix 1-20 on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341.

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第34页／PDF第43页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 34 / PDF p. 43. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

部分电路的负电流箭头：逐节点传递到端电压Signed boundary arrows in a partial circuit

题目原文Transcribed question

求题图1-19所示部分电路中A、B两点间的电压U_AB。Find U_AB between A and B in the partial circuit of Fig. 1-19.

左上节点L经3 Ω及8 V左正右负源到A；L经5 Ω到左下D，D经2 Ω到中节点C；C经10 Ω到开端B，并经2 A源流向A。L的外引线箭头向外标−1 A；D的外引线箭头向内标−1 A。Upper-left L connects through 3 Ω then an 8 V left-positive source to A. L connects through 5 Ω to lower-left D; D through 2 Ω to C. C connects through 10 Ω to open terminal B and through a 2 A source toward A. L has an outward boundary reference labeled −1 A; D has an inward boundary reference labeled −1 A.

本题图在书第34页／PDF第43页。The source figure is on printed p. 34 / PDF p. 43.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

负参考电流的实际方向；局部KCL；开端支路电流0；电压沿路径相加。Actual directions of negative referenced currents, local KCL, zero current at an open terminal and voltage summation along a path.
先把两个−1 A解释正确：上边界实际流入1 A，下边界实际流出1 A。A只有两条内部支路，2 A源流入A后必从顶支路返回L；B为开端，10 Ω压降为0。First interpret the boundary signs: 1 A actually enters at the upper cut and leaves at the lower cut. A has two internal branches, so the 2 A entering A returns through the upper branch to L. B is open, so the 10 Ω drop is zero.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

从A沿顶支路求相对电位Start at A and follow the upper branch

顶支路实际电流A→L为2 A。令8 V源左端为S，则V_S−V_L=2×3=6 V，且V_S−V_A=8 V，得到V_A=V_L−2 V。The upper branch carries 2 A from A to L. Calling the source’s left terminal S, V_S−V_L = 2×3 = 6 V and V_S−V_A = 8 V, so V_A = V_L−2 V.

VA=VL+6−8=VL−2

沿左侧和下侧求CFollow the left and lower resistors to C

L收到上边界1 A及顶支路2 A，故5 Ω向下3 A，V_D=V_L−15 V。D再由边界流出1 A，故2 Ω从D向C流2 A，V_C=V_D−4=V_L−19 V。L receives 1 A from the upper boundary and 2 A from the top branch, so 3 A flows down 5 Ω and V_D = V_L−15 V. At D, 1 A leaves through the boundary, leaving 2 A through 2 Ω toward C. Thus V_C = V_L−19 V.

I5Ω=3A,I2Ω=2A,VC=VL−15−4

利用B开端求U_ABUse the open B terminal

B没有外接通路，10 Ω中电流0，所以V_B=V_C。于是U_AB=V_A−V_B=(V_L−2)−(V_L−19)=17 V。B has no external path, so current in 10 Ω is zero and V_B = V_C. Hence U_AB = V_A−V_B = 17 V.

UAB=17V

U_AB=17 V（A比B高17 V）。U_AB = 17 V; A is 17 V above B.

易错点Common errors

  • 两个外引线都标−1 A，但箭头相反，所以实际方向也相反。Both boundary references are labeled −1 A, but their arrows differ, so their actual directions differ.
  • B是开端，10 Ω不是“流入1 A”的支路。B is an open terminal; the 10 Ω branch does not carry 1 A.

核验依据Verification

独立把两边界流量等效为D→L的1 A转移源，仅用于核验原部分电路。精确MNA得V_L=0、V_A=−2、V_D=−15、V_C=V_B=−19 V；10 Ω电流0，U_AB=17 V。内部电阻共耗散65 W，内部2 A源和8 V源提供34+16=50 W，边界净输入15 W，恰平衡。与附录第332页/PDF341页1-21一致。For independent validation only, represent the two boundary flows as an equivalent 1 A transfer D→L. Exact MNA gives V_L = 0, V_A = −2, V_D = −15 and V_C = V_B = −19 V, yielding zero 10 Ω current and U_AB = 17 V. Internal resistors dissipate 65 W; the internal sources supply 34+16 = 50 W and the boundary supplies the remaining 15 W. This matches appendix 1-21 on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341.
